1	\section{Experiments}\label{sec:experiments}
2	
3	\subsection{Experimental Setup}\label{sec:setup}
4	
5	\paragraph{Datasets.}
6	We evaluate on four English NER benchmarks spanning a difficulty gradient.
7	\textbf{SciERC} \cite{luan2018scierc} (551 test instances, 6 entity types) is the primary medium-difficulty test bed (${\approx}$65\% F1).
8	\textbf{Few-NERD} \cite{ding2021fewnerd} (INTRA coarse; 37{,}648 instances, 8 entity types, ${\approx}$75\% F1) provides a low-degeneracy contrast where LP selection succeeds (\S\ref{sec:selection_gap}).
9	\textbf{CoNLL-2003} \cite{tjongkimsang2003conll} (3{,}453 test sentences, 4 entity types, ${\approx}$91\% F1) serves as a ceiling-effect control.
10	\textbf{WNUT-17} \cite{derczynski2017wnut} (1{,}287 social media instances, 53.7\% zero F1) probes the signal-collapse boundary.
11	
12	\paragraph{Models and sampling.}
13	\textbf{Qwen3-8B} and \textbf{LLaMA-3.1-8B-Instruct} are LoRA fine-tuned using LLaMA Factory \cite{zheng2024llamafactory} (Appendix~\ref{app:training}; Few-NERD uses 3 epochs vs.\ 5 for others; Appendix~\ref{app:epoch_confound}).
14	$N{=}8$ samples at $T{=}1.0$ (primary) with vLLM constrained decoding \cite{willard2023guided}; $N {\in} \{16, 32, 64\}$ for scaling (Figure~\ref{fig:n_scaling}; Appendix~\ref{app:n_scaling}).
15	
16	\begin{figure}[t]
17	\centering
18	\includegraphics[width=\columnwidth]{figures/fig_n_scaling_mv.pdf}
19	\caption{MV $\Delta$F1 vs.\ $\log_2 N$ across three datasets (Qwen3-8B FT, seed${=}42$). Dotted gray: $\Delta{=}0$. Gains are dataset-dependent: persistently negative on Few-NERD, near zero on SciERC at higher $N$, marginally positive on CoNLL ($+0.6$\,pp at $N{=}64$).}
20	\label{fig:n_scaling}
21	\end{figure}
22	
23	%% ============================================================
24	\subsection{Quality Estimation Results}\label{sec:qe_results}
25	
26	\begin{table*}[t]

1	\section{Threshold Regime Analysis}\label{sec:theory_scaling}
2	
3	\subsection{Entity Construction as MBR Decoding}\label{sec:mbr_theory}
4	
5	Entity-level construction selects $\hat{y}_{\textup{MBR}} = \{e \in E_\cup : \hat{p}(e) > \alpha/(\alpha{+}1)\}$ under balanced set-overlap utility; $\alpha{=}1$ recovers majority vote (Proposition~\ref{prop:construction_mbr}; Table~\ref{tab:entity_construction}; Appendix~\ref{app:mbr_proof}).
6	Signal-derived weights $w_i$ yield quality-weighted MBR: $\hat{p}_w(e) = \sum_{i} w_i \, \mathbf{1}[e {\in} y_i] / \sum_{i} w_i$; weighting improves when $w_i$ correlates with instance quality but degrades otherwise (LP on SciERC: $\rho{\approx}0.2$; \S\ref{sec:prescriptive}), consistent with utility degradation on structured outputs \cite{eikema2025structured}.
7	We select $\varepsilon{=}0.05$ at the elbow of the compression curve, where LP-best selection advantage transitions from $+1.5$\,pp (compressed) to $+4.6$\,pp (non-compressed; Appendix~\ref{app:fewnerd_epsilon}).
8	
9	\subsection{Scaling and Inverse Scaling}\label{sec:scaling}
10	
11	Oracle F1 follows $F_1(N) = a - b \cdot N^{-c}$ ($R^2 > 0.999$; Figure~\ref{fig:oracle_scaling}): Few-NERD ($c{\approx}0.61$) scales comparably to reasoning tasks \cite{brown2024monkeys}, SciERC converges more slowly ($c{\approx}0.35$), yet confidence-based aggregation recovers ${<}1$\,pp---a persistent gap.
12	The gap persists across scales and families: scaling from 8B to 14B FT increases oracle headroom ($+1.2$\,pp) with minimal greedy gain ($+0.9$\,pp), and GPT-5.5 (200 Few-NERD instances, zero-shot) exhibits the same pattern---50\% degeneracy, SJ $\rho{=}0.346$, MV $+1.4$\,pp yet 6.8\,pp oracle headroom unrealized.\footnote{Of the 10.1\% of GPT-5.5 samples producing empty entity sets, 88\% correspond to gold-empty instances; true parse failures affect only 1.2\% of samples (19/1{,}600) and shift all reported metrics by ${<}0.5$\,pp. See Appendix~\ref{app:frontier_sc}.}

## 交叉分享：Reviewer 2 的完整审稿意见

请逐条回应 Reviewer 2 的判断，对每个要点标注 [AGREE] 或 [REBUT]（附理由）。重点关注你们之间的分歧点。

---

### Reviewer 2 的新发现问题

**[SHOULD-FIX] NEW-1: §2 Concurrent work (d) 与 §7.5 Exploratory 定位不一致**
§2 concurrent work 段落仍写 "(d) prescriptive---no constructive remedy vs. entity-level construction with MBR grounding"，但 §7.5 已标为 "(Exploratory)"，Table 1 Prescriptive 行标为 "Conditional (p_adj=0.055)"。§2 的对比语气暗示 prescriptive 是确定的优势，与全文其他位置的 hedging 不一致。

**[SHOULD-FIX] NEW-2: Discussion/Conclusion 降为 §7.5 段落，结构完整性受损**
Round 43 有独立的 §8 Discussion and Conclusion section，Round 44 将其降为 §7.5 内的 \paragraph。论文核心结论被嵌入标注为 "Exploratory" 的小节内，结构上暗示结论是探索性的。ACL/EMNLP 惯例是有独立 Discussion/Conclusion section。

**[MINOR] NEW-3: Ethics Statement 被移除**
Round 43 有完整 Ethics Statement，Round 44 完全删除。ARR 不强制但保留是好的学术实践。

**[MINOR] NEW-4: Limitations 覆盖面减少**
从 7 个要点压缩到 3 个，关键遗漏包括 mechanistic probe 跨模型迁移和评估指标局限性。

### Reviewer 2 的评分
- Overall: 3.0 (Findings) — 维持不变
- Excitement: 2.5/5
- Soundness: 3.5/5
- Reproducibility: 4/5

### 你与 R2 的关键分歧点

1. **Overall 评分差异**：你给 3.5 (borderline conference)，R2 给 3.0 (Findings)，差 0.5 分。
2. **Excitement 差异**：你给 3.5，R2 给 2.5，差 1.0 分——这是最大分歧。R2 认为现象"可预期"限制了兴奋度，你如何回应？
3. **Discussion 结构**：你认为 Discussion/Conclusion 合并"紧凑但可接受"，R2 认为这是 should-fix 级别的结构问题。
4. **NEW-1 concurrent work 措辞**：R2 标为 should-fix，你在初审中未提及。你是否同意这是一个叙事不一致问题？

请对以上每个分歧点和 R2 的每个新发现问题给出明确的 [AGREE] 或 [REBUT] 回应。

[Round 1 审稿请求]:
你是 Reviewer 1，负责 **方法严谨性 + 实验评估**。

## 投稿信息
- **Venue**: EMNLP 2026（通过 ARR 统一审稿）
- **Deadline**: 2026-06-15
- **格式**: Long paper，正文 8 页限制，参考文献不限，Limitations 必须有（不计页数）
- **模板**: ACL style（`\usepackage[review]{acl}`）
- **匿名**: Double-blind

## 论文文件
- LaTeX: `projects/struct_self_consist_ie/docs/paper/main.tex`
- PDF: `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0514_1/projects/struct_self_consist_ie/artifacts/review_round_45/submitted.pdf`

## 你的审查侧重

1. **Diagnostic Framework 有效性**：四个 failure mode（degeneracy, signal collapse 等）的定义是否严密、互斥、覆盖完整？诊断流程是否能被其他研究者复现？
2. **统计方法论**：多重比较校正是否恰当？统计检验的选择和报告是否符合标准？p-value / CI / effect size 的使用是否规范？Negative claims 是否有 power analysis 支持？
3. **实验设计**：数据集选择是否有代表性？seed 数量是否足够支撑统计推断？baseline 对比是否公平？ablation 是否充分？
4. **理论证明**：Proposition/Theorem 的假设是否合理？证明是否完备？bound 的紧致性如何？
5. **Entity construction（exploratory）**：实验结论是否与 exploratory 定位一致？是否有过度声称？

## 评分标准（EMNLP 2026 ARR）
5 = Consider for Award, 4.5 = Borderline Award, 4 = Conference, 3.5 = Borderline Conference, 3 = Findings, 2.5 = Borderline Findings, 2 = Resubmit next cycle, 1.5 = Resubmit after next cycle, 1 = Do not resubmit

## 输出格式
请按以下结构输出你的审稿意见：
1. **Summary**（2-3 句概述论文）
2. **Strengths**（逐条列出优点）
3. **Weaknesses**（逐条列出缺点，标注严重程度：Must-Fix / Should-Fix / Minor）
4. **Questions**（需要作者回答的问题）
5. **Missing References**（遗漏的重要相关工作）
6. **Overall Assessment**（评分 + 置信度 + 1-2 句总评）

1	---
2	name: review-reviewer-session-start
3	description: Review Reviewer session 启动初始化——检查论文格式、编译状态、材料清单。Review Reviewer 角色 session 启动时自动使用。
4	priority: 8
5	scope: global
6	tags:
7	- workflow
8	- review
9	- session
10	version: 2
11	roles: []
12	---
13	
14	# Review Reviewer Session 启动流程
15	
16	执行顺序：
17	1. 读取论文文件（`projects/{project}/paper/main.tex` 或类似路径）
18	2. 检查论文格式状态：
19	   - 页数是否在 venue 要求内（若已知 venue）
20	   - LaTeX 是否可编译（调用 `ssh_execute` 运行 `pdflatex` 或 `latexmk`）
21	   - 图表是否有标题和标签
22	3. 视觉检查：如果已有编译好的 PDF，使用 `paper-visual-review` skill 渲染关键页面（首页、图表页、参考文献页），检查排版溢出、公式渲染、图表质量等视觉问题
23	4. 检查投稿材料清单：
24	   - cover letter 是否存在
25	   - 补充材料是否准备
26	   - 参考文献格式是否正确
27	5. 向 Review Director 汇报（`review_reviewer_post_to_director`）：
28	   - 论文当前状态
29	   - 发现的格式问题（如有）
30	   - 视觉检查结果（如有）
31	   - 缺少的材料（如有）
32	
33	此流程在 Review Director 分配任务后执行。
34

# ACL Rolling Review Form - Scoring Dimensions

## 1. **Reviewer Confidence**
Scale: 1-5
- 5 = "Positive that my evaluation is correct"
- 4 = "Quite sure" with low likelihood of missed issues
- 3 = "Pretty sure, but there's a chance I missed something"
- 2 = "Willing to defend" but likely missed details
- 1 = "Not my area, or paper is very hard to understand"

## 2. **Soundness**
Scale: 1-5 (with .5 increments)
- 5 = "Excellent: This study is one of the most thorough"
- 4 = "Strong: sufficient support for all claims"
- 3 = "Acceptable: sufficient support for main claims"
- 2 = "Poor: main claims not sufficiently supported"
- 1 = "Major Issues: not sufficiently thorough for publication"

## 3. **Excitement**
Scale: 1-5 (with .5 increments)
- 5 = "Highly Exciting: would recommend to others"
- 4 = "Exciting: would mention to others"
- 3 = "Interesting: might mention to others"
- 2 = "Potentially Interesting: doesn't resonate with reviewer"
- 1 = "Not Exciting: wouldn't resonate with community"

## 4. **Overall Assessment**
Scale: 1-5 (with .5 increments)
- 5 = "Consider for Award" (top 2.5%)
- 4 = "Conference acceptance recommended"
- 3 = "Findings acceptance appropriate"
- 2 = "Resubmit after revisions"
- 1 = "Do not resubmit"

## 5. **Reproducibility**
Scale: 1-5
- 5 = "Easily reproduce results"
- 4 = "Mostly reproduce with minor variations"
- 3 = "Reproduce with some difficulty"
- 2 = "Hard to reproduce; limited data availability"
- 1 = "Cannot reproduce results"

## 6. **Datasets** (if applicable)
Scale: 1-5
- 5 = "Enabling" for research direction
- 4 = "Useful" for researchers
- 3 = "Potentially useful"
- 2 = "Documentary" value only
- 1 = "No usable datasets"

## 7. **Software** (if applicable)
Scale: 1-5
- 5 = "Enabling" for projects
- 4 = "Useful" for researchers
- 3 = "Potentially useful"
- 2 = "Documentary" value only
- 1 = "No usable software"

## 8. **Author Identity Knowledge**
Scale: 1-5 (anonymity assessment)
- 5 = Identified author via violations
